# 雅思单词 0001 · 水族馆；鱼缸
中文：n. 水族馆；鱼缸
单词：###aquarium###
搭配：###a public aquarium### 公共水族馆
例句：The kids loved the ###aquarium###, especially the amphibian section and an agile little crocodile.
译文：孩子们很喜欢水族馆，尤其是两栖动物区，还有一条灵活的小鳄鱼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0002 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0005 · 旷工，缺勤
中文：n. 旷工，缺勤
单词：###absenteeism###
搭配：###high rates of absenteeism### 高缺勤率
例句：###Absenteeism### among employees often reflects an underlying ailment or exhaustion, so managers should offer compassionate support rather than censure.
译文：员工缺勤往往反映出潜在的疾病或身心疲惫，所以管理者应该给予体贴的支持，而不是责备。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0006 -->

# 雅思单词 0010 · 艰巨的，费力的
中文：adj. 艰巨的，费力的
单词：###arduous###
搭配：###an arduous journey### 艰苦的旅程
例句：Had the climbers not turned back during their ###arduous### ascent, the avalanche would have buried them in the chasm below.
译文：要不是登山者在艰难的攀登途中及时折返，雪崩就会把他们埋进下面的深谷里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0014 · 删节，缩短
中文：v. 删节，缩短
单词：###abridge###
搭配：###an abridged edition### 删节版
例句：Could you ###abridge### your speech before the anthem? The acoustics here are poor, so half of it won't be audible.
译文：你能在奏国歌之前把讲话压缩一下吗？这里的音响效果很差，一半内容大家都会听不清。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0018 · 土著的，原住民的；原住民
中文：adj. 土著的，原住民的；n. 原住民
单词：###aboriginal###
搭配：###aboriginal culture### 原住民文化
例句：###Aboriginal### communities preserve ancestral knowledge of farming on arable land, yet many young people feel a growing alienation from this heritage.
译文：原住民社区保留着祖先在可耕地上耕作的知识，然而许多年轻人对这份传统越来越疏远。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0022 · 人类学家
中文：n. 人类学家
单词：###anthropologist###

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0026 · 关节炎
中文：n. 关节炎
单词：###arthritis###
搭配：###suffer from arthritis### 患关节炎
例句：Grandpa's ###arthritis### gets worse in winter and his asthma flares up, so he's moving to a sunny bungalow, his new abode by the sea.

# 雅思单词 0030 · 抗生素；抗菌的

单词：###antibiotic###
搭配：###a course of antibiotics### 一个疗程的抗生素
例句：Overuse of ###antibiotics### and antiseptic sprays helps bacterial strains resist treatment, and no single antidote exists for this crisis.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###autocratic###
搭配：###an autocratic ruler### 专制统治者
例句：However benevolent the ###autocratic### king appeared, his barbaric laws made ordinary citizens bear the brunt of every crisis.
译文：无论这位专制的国王看上去多么仁慈，他那些野蛮的法律都让普通百姓首当其冲，承受每一次危机的冲击。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###deter crime### 遏制犯罪

译文：体罚很少能阻止不良行为；研究表明它会适得其反，甚至可能让孩子更容易违法乱纪。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：这是复印件吗？人都会出错，弄混了情有可原，但他们要的是真护照，而且必须在有效期内。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0410 · 荒唐可笑的

译文：一些管理人员至今仍贬低远程办公、认为它松散懈怠，这实在荒唐，因为研究表明工作效率往往会提高。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0430 -->
# 雅思单词 0430 · 相互依存的
中文：adj. 相互依存的
单词：###interdependent###
搭配：###interdependent economies### 相互依存的经济体
例句：In an ###interdependent### world, insularity offers little protection, as a sudden influx of capital can inflate bubbles and leave banks insolvent.
译文：在相互依存的世界里，闭关自守起不了多大保护作用，因为资本的突然涌入可能吹大泡沫，让银行资不抵债。

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0539 · 对流

例句：Stop pestering me, Mum! I'm sluggish today, but I'll tidy my room after lunch; harassing me just makes me ###touchy###.
译文：妈，别再唠叨我了！我今天浑身没劲，不过午饭后会收拾房间的；你一直催只会让我更烦躁。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0599 -->
# 雅思单词 0599 · 甲烷，沼气
中文：n. 甲烷，沼气
单词：###methane###
搭配：###methane emissions### 甲烷排放
例句：###Methane### from livestock is a potent greenhouse gas, and overgrazing degrades pastures, so measures to mitigate both are urgent.
译文：牲畜排放的甲烷是一种强效温室气体，过度放牧又会让牧场退化，所以减轻这两方面影响的措施刻不容缓。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###of unknown provenance### 来源不明
例句：The scroll is thought to have been written by the late calligrapher, but verification of its ###provenance### must precede any posthumous publication.
译文：据认为这幅卷轴出自那位已故书法家之手，但在作为遗作出版之前，必须先核实其来源。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：别用那么嫌弃的眼神看我的面包——你看面包皮多有光泽！我是即兴配的方子，黄油也是自己搅的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0718 -->
# 雅思单词 0718 · 搅拌（牛奶制黄油）；翻腾

单词：###churn###
搭配：###churn butter### 搅制黄油
例句：Don't look at my bread with such disdain—look how glossy the crust is! I improvised the recipe and ###churned### the butter myself.
译文：别用那么嫌弃的眼神看我的面包——你看面包皮多有光泽！我是即兴配的方子，黄油也是自己搅的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：地质勘测显示，这个陨石坑是陨石撞击留下的，坑壁上露出富含燧石的层层沉积物。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：It was the vigilant ###coordinator###, not the guards, who first noticed the intrusion and alerted the warden.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0731 -->
# 雅思单词 0731 · 有读写能力的；精通……的
中文：adj. 有读写能力的；精通……的
单词：###literate###
搭配：###computer literate### 懂计算机的
例句：Being ###literate### today means reading text and tracing the dissemination of information, including semantic tricks that are barely identifiable.
译文：如今，有读写能力不仅意味着会读文字，还要能追踪信息的传播，包括辨别那些几乎难以察觉的语义陷阱。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0732 -->

例句：Being literate today means reading text and tracing the ###dissemination### of information, including semantic tricks that are barely identifiable.
译文：如今，有读写能力不仅意味着会读文字，还要能追踪信息的传播，包括辨别那些几乎难以察觉的语义陷阱。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0733 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0743 · 季节性的

搭配：###seasonal workers### 季节工
例句：The ###seasonal### monsoon brings vital rain to South Asia, but heavier storms now wash away topsoil and ravage crops.
译文：季节性季风给南亚带来至关重要的雨水，但如今更猛烈的暴雨会冲走表层土壤、毁坏庄稼。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0744 -->

搭配：###the monsoon season### 雨季
例句：The seasonal ###monsoon### brings vital rain to South Asia, but heavier storms now wash away topsoil and ravage crops.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###fertile topsoil### 肥沃的表土
例句：The seasonal monsoon brings vital rain to South Asia, but heavier storms now wash away ###topsoil### and ravage crops.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0746 -->

搭配：###be ravaged by war### 遭到战争蹂躏
例句：The seasonal monsoon brings vital rain to South Asia, but heavier storms now wash away topsoil and ###ravage### crops.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0747 -->
# 雅思单词 0747 · 流产；失败
中文：n. 流产；失败
单词：###miscarriage###
搭配：###a miscarriage of justice### 误判，冤案
例句：The prisoner, whose conviction was a ###miscarriage### of justice, was vindicated after a reputable lab showed police had mishandled evidence.
译文：这名囚犯被定罪是一起冤案，一家信誉良好的实验室证明警方处理证据不当之后，他终于洗清了冤屈。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0748 -->

单词：###vindicate###
搭配：###be vindicated by### 被……证明是对的
例句：The prisoner, whose conviction was a miscarriage of justice, was ###vindicated### after a reputable lab showed police had mishandled evidence.
译文：这名囚犯被定罪是一起冤案，一家信誉良好的实验室证明警方处理证据不当之后，他终于洗清了冤屈。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0749 -->

中文：adj. 声誉好的
单词：###reputable###
搭配：###a reputable firm### 信誉良好的公司
例句：The prisoner, whose conviction was a miscarriage of justice, was vindicated after a ###reputable### lab showed police had mishandled evidence.
译文：这名囚犯被定罪是一起冤案，一家信誉良好的实验室证明警方处理证据不当之后，他终于洗清了冤屈。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0750 -->
# 雅思单词 0750 · 处理不当
中文：v. 处理不当
单词：###mishandle###
搭配：###mishandle a crisis### 危机处理不当
例句：The prisoner, whose conviction was a miscarriage of justice, was vindicated after a reputable lab showed police had ###mishandled### evidence.
译文：这名囚犯被定罪是一起冤案，一家信誉良好的实验室证明警方处理证据不当之后，他终于洗清了冤屈。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0751 -->
# 雅思单词 0751 · 突然转向
中文：v. 突然转向
单词：###swerve###
搭配：###swerve to avoid### 急转避开
例句：I ###swerved### to avoid a squirrel, misjudged the curve and cracked my windscreen on a branch—what a mischance!
译文：我为了躲一只松鼠猛打方向盘，没判断好弯道，挡风玻璃被树枝砸裂了——真倒霉！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0752 -->

搭配：###misjudge the distance### 估错距离
例句：I swerved to avoid a squirrel, ###misjudged### the curve and cracked my windscreen on a branch—what a mischance!
译文：我为了躲一只松鼠猛打方向盘，没判断好弯道，挡风玻璃被树枝砸裂了——真倒霉！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0753 -->

单词：###windscreen###
搭配：###a cracked windscreen### 裂了的挡风玻璃
例句：I swerved to avoid a squirrel, misjudged the curve and cracked my ###windscreen### on a branch—what a mischance!
译文：我为了躲一只松鼠猛打方向盘，没判断好弯道，挡风玻璃被树枝砸裂了——真倒霉！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0754 -->
# 雅思单词 0754 · 不幸，厄运
中文：n. 不幸，厄运
单词：###mischance###
搭配：###by mischance### 不幸地
例句：I swerved to avoid a squirrel, misjudged the curve and cracked my windscreen on a branch—what a ###mischance###!
译文：我为了躲一只松鼠猛打方向盘，没判断好弯道，挡风玻璃被树枝砸裂了——真倒霉！

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0755 -->
# 雅思单词 0755 · 盛会，庆典；选美比赛
中文：n. 盛会，庆典；选美比赛
单词：###pageant###
搭配：###a historical pageant### 历史剧表演
例句：Critics call such summits a ###pageant### whose ostensible aim is cooperation but which merely legitimizes regimes that ignore inviolable rights.
译文：批评者称这类峰会只是一场盛大表演，表面上是为了合作，实际上却为那些无视不可侵犯的权利的政权披上合法外衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0756 -->

中文：adj. 表面上的，假装的
单词：###ostensible###
搭配：###the ostensible reason### 表面理由
例句：Critics call such summits a pageant whose ###ostensible### aim is cooperation but which merely legitimizes regimes that ignore inviolable rights.
译文：批评者称这类峰会只是一场盛大表演，表面上是为了合作，实际上却为那些无视不可侵犯的权利的政权披上合法外衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0757 -->
# 雅思单词 0757 · 使合法化
中文：v. 使合法化
单词：###legitimize###
搭配：###legitimize a regime### 使政权合法化
例句：Critics call such summits a pageant whose ostensible aim is cooperation but which merely ###legitimizes### regimes that ignore inviolable rights.
译文：批评者称这类峰会只是一场盛大表演，表面上是为了合作，实际上却为那些无视不可侵犯的权利的政权披上合法外衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：据信这座巨型雕像是历经几十年精雕细琢，用一整块石板凿刻而成的，每年春天都有一个舞蹈团在它旁边表演。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0832 -->

搭配：###reciprocate a favour### 回报恩惠

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：The report gives an overview of ###particulate### pollution and its pathology, showing how fine dust lodges in porous lung tissue.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a predisposition to### 有……的倾向
例句：The supposition that a genetic ###predisposition### decides success presupposes that training matters little, yet tenacious practice often wins.
译文：认为成功由遗传倾向决定的假设，预先认定了训练无足轻重，然而坚持不懈的练习往往更能取胜。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：###Mercantile### cities on navigable rivers intermixed goods and languages, and the interplay of cultures there reshaped art and religion.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0965 -->

# 雅思单词 0973 · 易变的，不稳定的；易挥发的

例句：###Volatile### fuel prices make planning hard, so airlines pinpoint savings wherever possible, and some resort to retrenchment to stay viable.
译文：燃油价格波动剧烈，让规划变得困难，所以航空公司想方设法找出可以节省的地方，有些甚至靠缩减开支来维持运营。

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 1021 · 取代，代替

搭配：###be superseded by### 被……取代
例句：Electric cars may soon ###supersede### petrol models as sales outstrip forecasts, so governments underwrite battery research, the pivot of the industry's future.
译文：随着销量超出预期，电动汽车可能很快取代燃油车，所以各国政府为电池研究提供资金保障，因为它是汽车业未来的关键。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：各国会储备粮食和燃料以备紧急情况，战时还可能征用私人囤积的物资，这是很少有人质疑的特权。

搭配：###economic uncertainty### 经济不确定性

译文：投机炒作推高了房价，泡沫一旦破裂，大规模抛售和不确定性可能让资产估值多年低迷。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1073 -->
# 雅思单词 1073 · 可以理解的

搭配：###an understandable mistake### 可以理解的错误
例句：It is ###understandable### that viewers find the storyline confusing, since the plot only unravels in the last episode, a trick to sustain suspense.
译文：观众觉得故事情节难以理解是可以理解的，因为情节直到最后一集才揭开，这是为了制造悬念的手法。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 1165 · 相互关系
中文：n. 相互关系
单词：###interrelationship###
搭配：###the interrelationship between### 之间的相互关系
例句：Ecologists examine the ###interrelationship### between subsoil, roots and terrestrial animals, since one disturbance can trickle through the whole system.
译文：生态学家研究底土、植物根系和陆生动物之间的相互关系，因为一处扰动就可能逐渐波及整个系统。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###teem with### 充满
例句：Although few of the snakes that ###teem### in this forest are venomous, hikers should wear boots and avoid thorny paths once winter dormancy ends.
译文：虽然这片森林里成群的蛇很少有毒，但冬眠期结束后，徒步者还是应该穿上靴子，避开荆棘丛生的小路。

<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：新开的面包店销量已经超过了竞争对手；它家的糕点轻盈得像仙品，所以我写了一篇极力推荐的点评，店主连声道谢！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1188 -->
# 雅思单词 1188 · 大量的，过多的
中文：adj. 大量的，过多的
单词：###profuse###
搭配：###profuse apologies### 再三道歉
例句：The new bakery already outsells its rivals; its pastries are ethereal, so I wrote a sterling review and got ###profuse### thanks!
译文：新开的面包店销量已经超过了竞争对手；它家的糕点轻盈得像仙品，所以我写了一篇极力推荐的点评，店主连声道谢！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1189 -->
# 雅思单词 1189 · 破坏性的，扰乱的
中文：adj. 破坏性的，扰乱的
单词：###disruptive###
搭配：###disruptive behaviour### 扰乱秩序的行为
例句：###Disruptive### or truant pupils often reflect disharmony at home, so teachers should involve parents rather than punish recalcitrant children.
译文：扰乱课堂或逃学的学生往往反映出家庭不和，所以老师应该让家长参与进来，而不是一味惩罚不听话的孩子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###sophisticated equipment### 精密设备

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1217 -->
# 雅思单词 1217 · 归纳的；感应的
中文：adj. 归纳的；感应的
单词：###inductive###
搭配：###inductive reasoning### 归纳推理
例句：What makes the novel gripping is the detective's ###inductive### reasoning, which spots each incongruity, protracts the tension and ends in a negation of every assumption.
译文：这部小说之所以引人入胜，是因为侦探的归纳推理：他捕捉每一处不协调，让紧张感不断延长，最后推翻了所有的假设。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###neurotic about### 对……神经过敏
例句：Stop being so ###neurotic### about germs! Health takes precedence, but this mania for hand gel is cracking your skin, and scolding guests isn't politic.
译文：别对细菌那么神经过敏了！健康固然要紧，可你这么狂用洗手液，手都裂了，再说训斥客人也不太得体。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###nautical###
搭配：###nautical miles### 海里
例句：Before satellites, sailors relied on ###nautical### charts to avoid rocks that protrude from the sea, and ships had wrought-iron superstructures to survive storms.
译文：在卫星出现之前，水手们依靠航海图来避开海面上突出的礁石，船只还配有锻铁上层结构以抵御风暴。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1257 -->

搭配：###converse with### 与……交谈
例句：Grandma would ###converse### for hours, extolling her remedies; her evidence was anecdotal, but her stance never changed.
译文：奶奶能跟人聊上好几个小时，大夸她的土方子；她的证据全凭道听途说，可她的立场从来没变过。

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1259 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1261 -->
# 雅思单词 1261 · 语料库；文集
中文：n. 语料库；文集
单词：###corpus###
搭配：###a corpus of English### 英语语料库
例句：Analysing a large ###corpus### to plumb everyday speech, linguists found that inversion after negatives is rare, making some textbook examples inapt.
译文：语言学家分析了一个大型语料库来探究日常口语，发现否定词后的倒装其实很少见，这让教科书里的一些例句显得不太恰当。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1262 -->

搭配：###plumb the depths of### 探究……的深处
例句：Analysing a large corpus to ###plumb### everyday speech, linguists found that inversion after negatives is rare, making some textbook examples inapt.
译文：语言学家分析了一个大型语料库来探究日常口语，发现否定词后的倒装其实很少见，这让教科书里的一些例句显得不太恰当。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1263 -->
# 雅思单词 1263 · 倒置，倒装；反转
中文：n. 倒置，倒装；反转
单词：###inversion###
搭配：###inversion of word order### 语序倒装
例句：Analysing a large corpus to plumb everyday speech, linguists found that ###inversion### after negatives is rare, making some textbook examples inapt.
译文：语言学家分析了一个大型语料库来探究日常口语，发现否定词后的倒装其实很少见，这让教科书里的一些例句显得不太恰当。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1264 -->

搭配：###an inapt comparison### 不恰当的比较
例句：Analysing a large corpus to plumb everyday speech, linguists found that inversion after negatives is rare, making some textbook examples ###inapt###.
译文：语言学家分析了一个大型语料库来探究日常口语，发现否定词后的倒装其实很少见，这让教科书里的一些例句显得不太恰当。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1265 -->
# 雅思单词 1265 · 有感知力的
中文：adj. 有感知力的
单词：###sentient###
搭配：###sentient beings### 有知觉的生物
例句：Scientists now deem octopuses ###sentient###, and genes in their DNA helix include enhancers controlling ink secretion and brain growth.
译文：科学家现在认为章鱼具有感知能力，它们 DNA 双螺旋中的基因包含控制喷墨分泌和大脑发育的增强子。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 1269 · 环绕，笼罩

单词：###wreathe###
搭配：###be wreathed in smiles### 满面笑容
例句：Our captain's face was ###wreathed### in smiles after we won the treble, though a scuffle among fans nearly cost everyone their sanity.
译文：我们赢得三冠王后，队长笑容满面，不过球迷之间的一场扭打差点让大家都失去理智。

# 雅思单词 1273 · 简洁的，简明的
中文：adj. 简洁的，简明的
单词：###succinct###
搭配：###a succinct summary### 简明的总结
例句：Career changers should write a ###succinct### application that highlights skills rather than perquisites, wiping the slate clean to gain traction with recruiters.
译文：转行的人应该写一份简明扼要的申请，突出技能而非以往的福利待遇，一切从头开始，才更容易打动招聘人员。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 1277 · 推进剂

单词：###propellant###
搭配：###rocket propellant### 火箭推进剂
例句：Formed when gas acts as a ###propellant### during the extrusion of lava, pumice is so light that it floats, and its layers overlie older rock.
译文：浮石是熔岩喷出时气体起推动作用而形成的，它轻得能浮在水面上，其岩层覆盖在较古老的岩石之上。

# 雅思单词 1281 · 缺点；（学校）记过，扣分

单词：###demerit###
搭配：###merits and demerits### 优缺点
例句：Don't let one ###demerit### fetter your ambition—it's an uphill climb, but you can recapture top marks next term!
译文：别让一次扣分束缚住你的志向——虽然要费点劲，但下学期你一定能重新拿回第一！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 1285 · 编码，把……译成密码

单词：###encode###
搭配：###encode data### 给数据编码
例句：After the hospital ###encoded### its natal records digitally, the paper files were consigned to storage and declared null and void.
译文：医院把出生记录数字化之后，纸质档案就被送进库房封存，并宣布作废。
<!-- /leiji-note -->
